Generated by tools/gcc_render.exe -triage (both backends, 25 s render at
48 kHz, TimGM6mb soundfont for MIDI).

Criterion: the file loads, plays, produces sound, AND the Gekko holds real

4937 files out of 5533, i.e. 89.2 %.

By backend:
  4827  module
   110  midi

THE CRITERION CHANGED, and that is the important point of this revision.

The previous version judged on the MEAN load of a 20 s render taken at the
START of the track. That is exactly the measurement that made the V2M look
tenable for two days, while its load rose a minute later (docs/STATUS.md 13).

We now judge on the WORST one-second WINDOW, over the whole duration of the
track. The left-hand column is still the render cost ON THIS HOST in
multiples of real time -- the larger the number, the lighter the file --
but it is the worst window, not the mean.

Projection onto the console, two measured anchors (campaign 2):

    midi     console worst window = 7300 / rt_host   (Fighting F -> 477)
    module   console worst window = 13300 / rt_host  (worst.xm   ->  12)

The heaviest of each category still in the pack:

    midi      386 per mille   PHROZEN CREW\PHROZEN CREW - 32 Card Bridge for Windows 1.6 nfo.mid
    module     93 per mille   !Others\Warez_Down - TopStyle 5.xx crk.it

NO file above 700 per mille. The margin is a factor of 2.5 at the worst
case, and this time it is measured on the right window.

Two caveats:
  - the .mid files are measured here WITHOUT the 64-voice ceiling the player
    applies; the midi anchor, on the other hand, is measured WITH it. The
    ceiling is not a constant factor, so the midi projection remains the
    most fragile.
  - a projection is for sorting, not for deciding within 50 per mille.
